  endtask

  // apply the operation held in the input signals; compare the handshake every cycle and the outputs when
  // the reference is ready. have_exp: also check the reference against a recorded expectation (phase 1).
  task automatic apply_op(input string src, input int idx, input bit have_exp, input logic [31:0] exp_res, input logic exp_c);
    int cycles; bit lat_bad; string d;
    @(negedge clk);
    if (ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
      d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b in the idle cycle, reference %b", ready_o, ready_ref);
      note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
    end
    enable_i = 1'b1;
    cycles = 0; lat_bad = 1'b0;
    do begin
      @(negedge clk);
      cycles++;
      if (!lat_bad && ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
        lat_bad = 1'b1;
        d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b at poll cycle %0d, reference %b (handshake timing differs)", ready_o, cycles, ready_ref);
        note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end while (ready_ref !== 1'b1 && cycles < 64);
    total_cycles += cycles;
    if (is_div(op)) div_cycles += cycles;
    total_checks++;
    if (ready_ref !== 1'b1) begin
      note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, "the reference did not assert ready_o within 64 cycles");
    end else begin
      if (result_o !== result_ref || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_o !== cmp_ref)) begin
        d = $sformatf("expected result=%08h cmp=%s got result=%08h cmp=%b", result_ref,
                      is_cmp(op) ? $sformatf("%b", cmp_ref) : "x", result_o, cmp_o);
        note_error("TB_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
      if (have_exp && (result_ref !== exp_res || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_ref !== exp_c))) begin
        d = $sformatf("reference result=%08h cmp=%b differs from the recorded expectation %08h cmp=%b", result_ref, cmp_ref, exp_res, exp_c);
        note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end
    enable_i = 1'b0;
  endtask

  // one random operation within the driven envelope
  task automatic random_op();
    logic [31:0] r0, r1, m;
    logic [6:0] o;
    int nvm;
    draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 57; o = OPS[r0]; nvm = vm_choices(o);
    op = o;
    draw(r1); r1 = r1 % 3;
    vm = (nvm == 1 || (nvm == 3 && r1 == 0)) ? 2'b00 : (r1 == 1) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11;
    bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0;
    a = rnd32(); b = rnd32(); c = rnd32();
    if (is_addfam(o)) begin
      draw(m); m = m % 100;
      if (is_rounding(o) || m < 40) begin draw(r0); bma = r0[4:0]; draw(r1); bmb = r1[4:0]; end
      if (o == OP_SUB && vm == 2'b10 && m >= 40 && m < 55) sbr = 1'b1;                       // pv.subrotmj
      else if (!is_rounding(o) && vm != 2'b11 && m >= 55 && m < 70) begin cpx = 1'b1; draw(r0); csh = r0[1:0]; bma = '0; bmb = '0; end
    end
    if (is_bitmanip(o)) begin
      draw(r0); bma = r0[4:0]; draw(r1); bmb = r1[4:0];
      draw(m); m = m % 10;
      if (o != 7'h2b && o != 7'h2c && o != 7'h49 && m < 7) b = {27'd0, bmb};                 // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS: b = bmb as the decoder does
      if (o == 7'h49) begin draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 32; b = (m < 5) ? r0 : {27'd0, bmb}; end              // BREV: left shift by b[4:0]
    end
    if (is_shift(o) && vm == 2'b00) b = shift_amt32();
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS || o == OP_INS) begin draw(r0); ive = r0[1:0]; end
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS) c = '0;
    if (o == OP_ABS) begin b = '0; draw(m); m = m % 5; if (vm == 2'b10 && m == 0) cpx = 1'b1; end   // complex conjugate on 16-bit lanes
    if (o == 7'h16 || o == 7'h17) begin draw(m); draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 32; if (m[0] == 1'b0) b = (32'h1 << r0) - 1; end   // CLIP bounds 2^k-1
    if (is_div(o)) begin
      draw(m); m = m % 10;
      if (m < 4) begin draw(r0); r0 = 1 + r0 % 32; a = with_bits(int'(r0)); end                   // divisor of any bit length
      else if (m < 6) begin draw(a); a = a % 64; end                                              // small divisor, long division
      else if (m < 7) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); b = a * (r0 % 16) + r1 % 8; end                   // near-exact multiple
      if (is_sdiv(o)) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); if (r0[0]) a = -a; if (r1[0]) b = -b; end
    end
  endtask

  // ---- stimulus ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  int fd, n, vidx, k, kb, mb, pass;
  logic [31:0] r0, r1;
  logic [3:0]  cchk, exp_cmp;
  logic [31:0] exp_result;
  string line;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0; total_errors = 0; shown = 0; total_cycles = 0; div_cycles = 0; aborted = 1'b0; rs = 32'h2545F491;
    rst_n = 1'b0; enable_i = 1'b0; ex_ready_i = 1'b1;
    op = '0; a = '0; b = '0; c = '0; vm = '0; bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0;
    repeat (5) @(negedge clk);
    rst_n = 1'b1;
    @(negedge clk);

    // 1. the vector file
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) $fatal(1, "cannot open vectors.dat");
    vidx = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd) && !aborted) begin
      if ($fgets(line, fd) == 0) break;
      if (line.len() == 0 || line[0] == "#" || line[0] == "\n") continue;
      n = $sscanf(line, "%h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h",
                  op, vm, bma, bmb, ive, cpx, sbr, csh, a, b, c, cchk, exp_result, exp_cmp);
      if (n != 14) continue;
      apply_op("file", vidx, 1'b1, exp_result, exp_cmp[0]);
      vidx++;
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    // 2. the divider sweep: divisor bits x dividend bits x opcode, two passes
    vidx = 0;
    for (pass = 0; pass < 2 && !aborted; pass++)
      for (k = 53; k < 57 && !aborted; k++)
        for (kb = 1; kb <= 32 && !aborted; kb++)
          for (mb = 1; mb <= 32 && !aborted; mb++) begin
            op = OPS[k]; vm = '0; bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0; c = '0;
            a = with_bits(kb);                        // the divisor (operand_a_i)
            b = with_bits(mb);                        // the dividend (operand_b_i)
            if (is_sdiv(op)) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); if (r0[0]) a = -a; if (r1[0]) b = -b; end
            apply_op("sweep", vidx, 1'b0, '0, 1'b0);
            vidx++;
          end

    // 3. random operations within the envelope
    for (vidx = 0; vidx < N_RANDOM && !aborted; vidx++) begin
      random_op();
      apply_op("random", vidx, 1'b0, '0, 1'b0);
    end

    $display("TB_CYCLES total=%0d", total_cycles);
    $display("TB_DIV_CYCLES total=%0d", div_cycles);
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

module cv32e40p_alu__ref
(
    input logic               clk,
    input logic               rst_n,

  // DIV DIVU REM REMU\n    endcase\n  end\n  assign result_o = r;\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div u_div (\n      .Clk_CI(clk), .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI(operand_b_i), .OpB_DI(div_bshift), .OpBShift_DI(div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 6'd0), .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI(op[1:0]), .InVld_SI(div_valid), .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready), .Res_DO(result_div));\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (input wire [31:0] in_i, output wire [5:0] result_o);\n  wire [15:0][1:0] c1; wire [7:0][2:0] c2; wire [3:0][3:0] c3; wire [1:0][4:0] c4;\n  genvar l;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l = l + 1) begin : g1\n      assign c1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (l = 0; i < LEN; i = i + 1)\n      if (in_i[i]) idx = i[4:0];\n  end\n  assign first_one_o = idx;\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  wire                   Clk_CI, Rst_RBI,\n    input  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0]     OpA_DI, OpB_DI,\n    input  wire [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  wire                   OpBIsZero_SI, OpBSign_SI,\n    input  wire [1:0]             OpCode_SI,\n    input  wire                   InVld_SI, OutRdy_SI,\n    output wire                   OutVld_SO,\n    output wire [C_WIDTH-1:0]     Res_DO\n);\n  localparam IDLE = 2'd0, DIVIDE = 2'd1, FINISH = 2'd2;\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN, AReg_DP, AReg_DN, BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n  wire RemSel_SP, CompInv_SP, ResInv_SP, RemSel_SN, CompInv_SN, ResInv_SN;\n  wire [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  wire [1:0] State_SP, State_SN;\n  wire LoadEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, CntZero_S;\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D, AddOut_D, AddTmp_D, BMux_D, OutMux_D, ResReg_DP_rev;\n\n  assign PmSel_S = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[C_WIDTH-1] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n  assign AddMux_D = LoadEn_S ? (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15:8]  : shuffle_r1_in[7:0]);\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15:8]  = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15:8]  : shuffle_r0[15:8];\n  assign shuffle_result[7:0]   = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[7:0]   : shuffle_r0[7:0];\n  assign pack_result[31:24] = shuffle_through[3] ? shuffle_result[31:24] : operand_c_i[31:24];\n  assign pack_result[23:16] = shuffle_through[2] ? shuffle_result[23:16] : operand_c_i[23:16];\n  assign pack_result[15:8]  = shuffle_through[1] ? shuffle_result[15:8]  : operand_c_i[15:8];\n  assign pack_result[7:0]   = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[7:0]   : operand_c_i[7:0];\n\n  // ---------------- result mux ----------------\n  reg [31:0] r;\n  always @* begin\n    case (op)\n      AND_: r = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      OR_:  r = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      XOR_: r = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n      ADD, ADDR, ADDU, ADDUR, SUB, SUBR, SUBU, SUBUR, SLL, SRL, SRA, ROR: r = shift_result;\n      BINS, BEXT, BEXTU: r = bextins_result;\n      BCLR: r = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n      BSET: r = operand_a_i | bmask;\n      BREV: r = reverse_result;\n      SHUF, SHUF2, PCKLO, PCKHI, EXT, EXTS, cmp_result[3]};\n      FF1, FL1, CLB, CNT: r = {26'h0, bitop_result};\n      default: r = result_div;\n    endcase\n  end\n  assign result_o = r;\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div u_div (\n      .Clk_CI(clk), .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI(operand_b_i), .OpB_DI(div_bshift), .OpBShift_DI(div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 6'd0), .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI(op[1:0]), .InVld_SI(div_valid), .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready), .Res_DO(result_div));\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (input wire [31:0] in_i, output wire [5:0] result_o);\n  wire [15:0][1:0] c1; wire [7:0][2:0] c2; wire [3:0][3:0] c3; wire [1:0][4:0] c4;\n  genvar l;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l = l + 1) begin : g1\n      assign c1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (l = 0; l < 8; l = l + 1) begin : g2\n      assign c2[l] = {1'b0, c1[2*l]} + {1'b0, c1[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (l = 0; l < 4; l = l + 1) begin : result_o = {1'b0, c4[0]} + {1'b0, c4[1]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(parameter LEN = 32) (\n    input  wire [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output wire [4:0]     first_one_o,\n    output wire           no_ones_o\n);\n  assign no_ones_o = ~|in_i;\n  reg [4:0] idx;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    idx = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < LEN; i = i + 1)\n      if (in_i[i]) idx = i[4:0];\n  end\n wire                   InVld_SI, OutRdy_SI,\n    output wire                   OutVld_SO,\n    output wire [C_WIDTH-1:0]     Res_DO\n);\n  localparam IDLE = 2'd0, DIVIDE = 2'd1, FINISH = 2'd2;\n  wire [ASSISTANT]
## Summary

**Final state: PASS was only achieved by the unmodified `starting_point.sv` (cost 6.456e5). My rewritten `design.sv` was still FAILING at the step limit.**

### 1. Approaches tried

I rewrote the ALU from scratch in `design.sv` rather than incrementally editing the starting point, targeting these structural savings:
- **Single adder for add/sub/ABS/compare**: one 36-bit segmented adder with per-lane carry-in slots, deriving all lane compares (`lt8/lt16/lt32`) from its carry bits instead of the upstream's separate 4-lane 9-bit signed comparators.
- **Signed compare via the "sign-bit OR" trick**: `lt_signed = (a_sign & ~b_sign) | (~(a^b signs) & lt_unsigned)`, avoiding operand pre-muxing.
- **One lane-aware barrel shifter** (5 stages of 1/2/4/8/16) with per-lane fill and rotate wiring, replacing upstream's 64-bit shifter *plus* separate 16-bit and 8-bit per-lane shifters.
- **Priority-encoder sharing** for FF1/FL1/CLB/div_shift via a single loop-based encoder.
- **Kept the divider cycle-exact** but merged DIVIDE/FINISH control.

### 2. What worked
- Correct: divider handshake/latency (ready timing matched from the first working build), popcount, min/max, adder lane segmentation, CLIP, shuffle/pack (after transcribing upstream's tables exactly), and the borrow-based compare skeleton.
- Debugging via TB error messages was very effective — each error line pinpointed the exact family that was broken.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- **`ready_o` stuck low**: assigning to an output declared as `wire` procedurally (Verilator PROCASSWIRE). Fixed with an internal `reg` + `assign`.
- **Priority encoder polarity**: returned the *highest* set bit; FF1 needs the *lowest* (loop must run downward).
- **Shifter direction**: my `bstage` initially shifted the wrong way; also missing sign-fill at 32-bit-only stages, and wrong fill-bit for the m32/m16 lane boundary bytes. Multiple iterations.
- **BREV**: I applied the group-reverse to `brev(shift_result)`, double-reversing; the two full bit-reversals cancel, so it must use the raw barrel output.
- **EXT 16-bit table**: one wrong literal (`1'b0` vs `1'b1` in `shuffle_byte_sel[1]`).
- **Compare carry polarity (still open at the end)**: `carry(a+b+1)` is *not* `a≥b`. Making the adder subtract for compare ops (`is_cmp`) was the right idea, but the borrow then flows across lane boundaries through the carry slots differently and I ran out of steps validating it — the final run still had all-zero compare results.
- Tool quirks: `automatic` variables inside functions break Yosys; `{8{x},8{y}}` concatenation is illegal.

### 4. Lessons / next time
- **Start from the working file and make small, individually-verified edits.** A full rewrite of 57 opcodes with SIMD lanes has too many interacting details to debug inside a 60-step budget; the "many small diffs, evaluate each" strategy the prompt recommends is genuinely the right one.
- **Verify a candidate rewrite against the reference ALU with a tiny self-written smoke test** (or better: run the provided TB early and often) rather than reasoning purely from the spec text.
- **Write comparison logic in its obvious subtract form from the start** (`lt = ~carry(a + ~b + 1)`) and test it standalone before integrating.
- Transcribing lookup tables (shuffle/EXT/INS) should be done by copy-paste from the reference, never retyped.
- Budget-wise: I spent ~10 steps on file-reading and ~15 on a fundamentally broken first draft; a correct-but-unoptimized starting point passed on step 6, so the wasted margin was the difference between shipping an optimization and shipping nothing.
